### [1] [Asymptotic safety is not generic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07002)
*Francesco Del Porro,Francesco Ferrarin,Alessia Platania*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究SU(2)和SU(3)手征微扰理论（具有与量子引力相似的不可重整性和非线性相互作用），在四阶导数截断下运用函数重整化群方法，未发现非平庸紫外不动点；由此推断渐近安全并非普遍性质，而是特定系统的特殊特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究渐近安全是否为普遍现象——即非平庸紫外不动点（如Reuter不动点）在具有类似不可重整性与非线性结构的理论中是否普遍存在。

Method: 采用函数重整化群（FRG）方法，在四阶导数截断下系统分析SU(2)和SU(3)手征微扰理论的β函数零点结构，以搜寻非平庸紫外不动点。

Result: 在所考察的手征模型中，未发现相互作用的紫外不动点；所有解均对应高斯型（自由）不动点或无物理意义的复不动点。

Conclusion: 渐近安全并非由不可重整性或非线性相互作用自动导致的通用性质，而依赖于理论的具体对称性、场内容与动力学结构，因此是特殊而非普适的紫外完成机制。

Abstract: Asymptotically safe quantum gravity proposes a non-perturbative ultraviolet completion of gravity in which the high-energy behavior is controlled by an ultraviolet fixed point---the Reuter fixed point---for which there is robust evidence from a wide range of settings and truncations. The existence of asymptotic safety requires non-trivial zeros of beta functions, raising the question of how generic such fixed-point solutions are. In this work, we tackle this issue by investigating chiral perturbation theories. We focus on SU(2) and SU(3) chiral models, which share key features with gravity such as perturbative non-renormalizability or nonlinear interactions, but which are known to admit a different ultraviolet completion via new degrees of freedom. Using the functional renormalization group, we study these theories within a quartic-derivative truncation and find no evidence for an interacting fixed point. This result suggests that asymptotic safety is not generic, but rather a feature pertaining to certain special systems.

</details>


### [2] [On Entanglement Dynamics in Holographic Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07010)
*Hanzhi Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过AdS/CFT对偶研究强耦合量子场论中纠缠熵（EE）与反射熵（RE）的实时演化，涵盖二维CFT中的SSD变形、混沌系统的膜描述、张力退化与非退化机制、BTZ黑洞内解与Kasner宇宙的全息实现，以及高维反射熵膜理论在多体纠缠信号中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵在含时相互作用系统中难以计算；需发展适用于强耦合、非平衡、混沌QFT的普适动力学框架，并统一描述EE与RE的动力学行为。

Method: 采用AdS/CFT全息对偶作为核心工具；结合精确可解2d CFT模型（含正弦平方形变）、膜理论（entanglement membrane）、几何重构（foliation/horizon分析）、全息重整化与bulk reconstruction技术；引入反射熵的广义膜自由度以刻画多体量子关联。

Result: 1) SSD驱动的EE演化对应于AdS₃中foliation改变并生成新视界；2) 2d CFT中反射熵需额外膜自由度，而相关变形诱导非退化张力，对应带标量毛的BTZ黑洞与内部Kasner宇宙；3) 构建了高维反射熵的膜有效理论；4) 揭示scrambling动力学产生具有丰富结构的多体纠缠信号。

Conclusion: 纠缠与反射熵的动力学可通过几何化膜理论统一描述，其张力性质（退化/非退化）由边界理论的共形性破缺程度及全息bulk结构（如内部奇点几何）决定；该框架为非平衡量子引力与信息动力学提供了微观可实现的半经典桥梁。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy (EE) is central to modern theoretical physics, yet its calculation in interacting theories with nontrivial time dependence remains challenging. In this thesis, we investigate the real-time dynamics of EE in strongly coupled quantum field theories (QFTs) using the Anti-de Sitter/Conformal Field Theory (AdS/CFT) correspondence. We first examine EE evolution in an exactly solvable two-dimensional conformal field theory (CFT) with an inhomogeneous sine-square deformation (SSD). We argue that the unconventional time evolution driven by the SSD Hamiltonian can be viewed as a change in spacetime foliation, producing new horizons in the dual three-dimensional bulk. Holographic EE calculations agree with CFT results in all comparable cases. We then study entanglement dynamics in generic chaotic quantum many-body systems. In the limit of late time and large regions, these dynamics are believed to admit an effective entanglement membrane description. In 2d CFTs, a degenerate membrane tension reproduces several entropy dynamics results, unlike the non-degenerate tension in higher dimensions. Using holography, we show that capturing reflected entropy in 2d CFT requires an additional degree of freedom in the membrane description. We further show that a conventional non-degenerate tension emerges when the CFT undergoes a relevant deformation, holographically dual to a planar Banados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole with scalar hair and an interior Kasner universe. We then develop a membrane effective theory for reflected entropy dynamics in higher dimensions. Finally, we apply the membrane theories for reflected entropy to study the time evolution of multipartite entanglement signals. We found that scrambling dynamics generate multipartite entanglement with rich structures.

</details>


### [3] [Constraint Variance Identity and the Matter-Geometry Fluctuation Relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07015)
*Yuki Yokokura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了物质-几何涨落关系，建立了时空钟涨落与物质涨落之间的动力学联系，并导出了物质能量与关系时间之间的不确定性关系，表明时空的稳定存在依赖于物质的量子涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力框架下时空结构的稳定性及其与物质量子涨落之间的基本关联。

Method: 基于minisuperspace中可分离哈密顿量的量子哈密顿约束为零的条件，结合Born-Oppenheimer近似，分析其方差消失所导出的基本恒等式。

Result: 得到Matter-Geometry Fluctuation Relation；导出clock对总量子噪声的定量稳定性条件；获得物质能量与关系时间之间的不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 时空的稳定存在以物质的量子涨落为必要条件，揭示了物质量子性对经典时空涌现的关键作用。

Abstract: The quantum Hamiltonian constraint vanishes for physical states. For separable Hamiltonians in minisuperspace, its vanishing variance yields a fundamental identity. Under the Born-Oppenheimer approximation, we derive the Matter-Geometry Fluctuation Relation, dynamically linking spacetime clock fluctuations to matter fluctuations. This establishes a quantitative stability condition for the clock against total quantum noise. Finally, we obtain an uncertainty relation between matter energy and relational time. The stable existence of spacetime thus demands quantum fluctuations in matter.

</details>


### [4] [Charged AdS$_{3}$ black branes as marginally deformed two-dimensional CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07019)
*Suting Zhao,Gabriel Arenas-Henriquez,Yizhou Lu,Hamed Adami,René Meyer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₃黑洞的边界关联函数如何编码重正化欧氏作用量对电荷的依赖性；发现电荷密度展开系数由Catalan数控制，二次项由标量两点函数与源的平移对称性决定Weyl反常，四次项由树图引力子交换贡献，并通过红外截断积分复现；高阶项对应所有连通树图的积分和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS/CFT中边界关联函数如何编码体理论中带电黑brane的热力学势（即重正化欧氏作用量）对电荷的非线性依赖，特别是当规范场具有对数渐近行为、对偶于边缘算符时的动力学结构。

Method: 在三维Einstein-Maxwell理论中，利用AdS₃黑膜的规范/引力对偶，结合共形边界上的源-算符映射、平移对称性约束、Weyl反常分析、以及树级Witten图计算（含红外截断），系统展开重正化作用量关于电荷密度的幂级数，并比对关联函数贡献。

Result: 重正化欧氏作用量在固定温度下关于电荷密度为偶函数，其展开系数由Catalan数精确给出；二次项由对偶标量两点函数与源的shift symmetry确定，编码物质Weyl反常及对数温依赖；四次项由树级graviton交换的连通四点函数经线性源卷积并红外截断后复现；任意n阶系数对应所有n点连通树图的积分和。

Conclusion: 边界连通关联函数（尤其是两点与四点）完整编码了AdS₃带电黑brane作用量的电荷依赖结构，Catalan数作为普适组合因子出现，揭示了对数渐近规范场下边缘理论中非平凡源动力学与体引力摄动之间的深层组合与几何对应。

Abstract: We study how boundary correlation functions encode the charge dependence of the renormalised Euclidean action of an AdS$_3$ black brane. In three-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell theory, the Maxwell field has logarithmic asymptotics and is locally dual to a massless pseudoscalar. In the canonical ensemble, the charge density fixes the slope of a spatially linear source for the dual marginal operator. At fixed temperature, the classical action is even in the charge density due to reflection symmetry $\Phi\to-\Phi$, and its charge expansion coefficients are governed by Catalan numbers. We compute the quadratic and quartic terms from connected correlation functions around the neutral brane. The scalar two-point function and the shift symmetry of its source determine the matter Weyl anomaly, which fixes the logarithmic temperature dependence of the quadratic term in the charge expansion. At quartic order, the connected four-point function comes from tree-level graviton exchange. Integrating it against the linear source, with an infrared cutoff on the overall scale of the integral, reproduces the quartic coefficient of the action per unit length. At each higher order, the corresponding Catalan coefficient predicts the integrated sum of all connected tree-level Witten diagrams.

</details>


### [5] [Finite is not enough: Triviality bounds in nonlocal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07026)
*Fabian Wagner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出紫外有限理论不等价于紫外完备，挑战了仅靠消除紫外发散就支持量子引力中时空离散化的观点；通过在含紫外压制形因子的四维φ⁴理论中推导单圈动量减除流，发现非局域尺度之上跑动被冻结，但Landau极点仍可能在压制生效前出现；为保证全能标微扰可控，得到红外耦合的严格上界，该上界在所考虑模型中变化仅约1%，并推测离散时空模型亦有类似约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑仅以消除紫外发散为依据支持时空离散化的逻辑，澄清紫外有限性与紫外完备性的概念区分，并探究非局域理论的可预测性边界。

Method: 在四维ϕ⁴理论中引入紫外压制形因子（如指数或有理函数型非局域核），计算单圈动量减除方案下的β函数流，分析跑动行为、Landau极点出现条件及微扰一致性约束。

Result: 证明即使理论紫外有限，仍可能因Landau极点而缺乏紫外完备性；导出红外耦合g_IR的普适性上界，其对不同形因子形式弱依赖（变化<1%）；表明要求紫外完备性可显著提升非局域量子场论的可预测能力。

Conclusion: 紫外有限性不足以保证紫外完备性；时空离散化若仅以消除紫外发散为目标，则其理论动机被削弱；真正提升可预测性的是对紫外完备性的要求，这为非局域/离散量子引力模型提供了新的约束范式。

Abstract: An ultraviolet-finite theory need not be ultraviolet complete. This observation weakens the case for spacetime discreteness in quantum gravity if that case rests on removing ultraviolet divergences alone. We arrive at this observation by deriving the one-loop momentum-subtraction flow in four-dimensional $\phi^4$ theories with ultraviolet-suppressing form factors. The suppression freezes the running above the nonlocality scale, but the theory can hit a Landau pole before suppression sets in. We obtain a bound on the infrared coupling by requiring perturbative control at all scales. This bound varies only at the percent level across the examples we consider. We expect similar bounds in models of discrete spacetime. Thus, requiring ultraviolet completeness improves the predictivity of nonlocal theories.

</details>

Method: 基于Butter等人的对数构造法，将高阶导数共形原初（超）场嵌入共形张量 calculus 框架，结合Wess-Zumino一致性条件与超共形不变性约束，导出对应反常的有效作用量。

Result: 得到任意偶维Weyl反常、四维N=1/N=2超Weyl反常及二维(p,q)（p,q≤2）超Weyl反常的显式、局部且协变的Wess-Zumino作用量表达式。

Conclusion: 对数构造法为系统生成共形与超共形反常的有效作用量提供了普适工具，强化了高阶导数共形原初场与反常几何之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We apply the logarithm construction of higher-derivative conformal primary (super) fields proposed by Butter et al. to derive simple Wess-Zumino actions for: (i) Weyl anomalies in arbitrary even dimensions; (ii) $\mathcal{N} =1$ and $\mathcal{N}=2$ super-Weyl anomalies in four dimensions; and (iii) $(p,q)$ super-Weyl anomalies in two dimensions, with $p,q \leq 2$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有非相对论对称性的三维引力理论中具有明确热力学意义的黑洞性解，并揭示其守恒荷结构与极值条件。

Method: 基于三维伽利略反德西特时空的对偶扩展牛顿-胡克代数，构造拓扑规范理论；通过欧氏路径积分与正则性条件确定热力学参数；分析守恒荷代数及界约束。

Result: 获得首个具四守恒荷（能量、角动量、自旋、静质量）的三维Galilean AdS引力精确解；证明自旋满足|S| ≤ M；导出温度T、角速度Ω等热力学量；发现极值黑洞性态对应S = M的饱和情形。

Conclusion: 该解确立了非相对论极限下引力黑洞性质的严格实现，表明伽利略共形对称性可支撑具有完备热力学的黑洞性构型，且其极值性由自旋-静质量不等式刻画。

Abstract: We report a novel solution of three-dimensional Galilean Anti-de Sitter gravity. The theory can be formulated as a topological gauge theory invariant under the doubly extended Newton-Hooke group in three dimensions. The solution possesses four conserved charges: energy, angular momentum, spin and rest mass. Interestingly, the magnitude of the spin turns out to bounded from above by the rest mass. The solution exhibits the thermal properties that define a black hole configuration in terms of its gauge theoretical description. Temperature, angular velocity and other chemical potentials are obtained from suitable regularity conditions associated with the topology of the Euclidean continuation of the solution. At zero temperature, an extreme configuration is shown to emerge provided the bound between the spin and the rest mass saturates.

</details>


### [8] [Fermion$\text{-}$Monopole Scattering: A Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07001)
*Hao Geng,Zekai Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了无质量手性费米子在最小带电BPS 't Hooft-Polyakov单极子背景下的散射问题，通过构建一个统一的低能费米子-转子（fermion-rotor）有效描述，澄清了先前不同理论方案之间的关系；引入辅助标量场构造对偶理论实现精确量子化，证明转子顶点具有零能连续谱，并识别其为出射费米子的twist dressing；同时直接从紧致转子绕数构型激发的费米子零模计算反常关联函数，从而统一了转子动力学、扭曲出射态与零模起源三者，消除了既有方案间的表观矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有关于单极子散射中出射态定义、能量守恒及反常散射振幅的不同方案之间缺乏清晰联系甚至存在表观矛盾的问题。

Method: 基于单极子背景推导低能费米子-转子耦合模型，全程保留显式费米子算符；引入解耦辅助标量构造可精确量子化的对偶理论；利用转子紧致性及其绕数构型分析零模结构与红外交换关系；结合谱分析与twist dressing机制识别出射态。

Result: 1) 转子顶点具有下至零能的连续谱；2) 其红外交换关系确认其为出射费米子的twist dressing；3) 反常费米子关联函数可直接由绕数诱导的费米子零模导出；4) 所有既有方案在共同适用区域内自洽统一。

Conclusion: 单极子散射中的转子动力学、扭曲出射态与零模反常三者本质统一；先前看似冲突的方案实为同一物理图像在不同表象或近似下的体现，该工作提供了自洽的低能有效框架。

Abstract: We revisit the scattering of massless chiral fermions by a minimally charged BPS 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole. Existing proposals emphasize different aspects of this process and their connections are often unclear, giving rise to apparent conflicts concerning the outgoing states, energy conservation, and anomalous scattering amplitudes. We clarify the connection between different proposals within a common low-energy fermion-rotor description. Starting from the monopole background, we derive the rotor coupling and retain explicit fermion operators throughout the analysis. By adjoining a decoupled auxiliary scalar, we construct a dual theory that permits exact quantization. We show that the rotor vertex has a continuous spectral support down to zero energy and that its infrared exchange relations identify it as the twist dressing of the outgoing fermion. We also compute anomalous fermion correlators directly from fermion zero modes generated by winding configurations of the compact rotor. These results connect rotor dynamics, twisted outgoing states, and the zero-mode origin of anomalous correlators, resolving apparent conflicts and showing how the existing proposals fit into a mutually consistent picture within their common regime of applicability.

</details>


### [9] [Constraining the spectral gap of 2d CFTs just above c=1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07011)
*Nathan Benjamin,Kevin Langhoff,Wei Li,Jesse Thaler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用模自守bootstrap方法，数值研究了中心荷c>1的紧致幺正二维共形场论（2d CFT）的空间，重点约束其能谱间隙Δ_gap（即非真空原初算符的最小标度维数），并引入原初算符简并度为正整数这一关键整性约束；结果表明，在c→1⁺时该约束显著收紧上界，暗示Δ_gap可能在c=1处消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解紧致幺正2d CFT的分类与极限行为，特别是c→1⁺时谱结构的连续性与离散性问题；现有bootstrap界限未充分纳入整数量子化条件，导致物理可实现理论的空间被高估。

Method: 采用模bootstrap框架，结合原始（primal）谱搜索与对偶（dual）排除法；核心创新是强加原初算符的简并度为正整数（而非仅非负实数），并通过数值线性规划/半定规划实施约束优化。

Result: 在c−1=3×10⁻³处，整性约束将严格上界从Δ_gap≲0.5收紧至Δ_gap<0.041；数值证据支持Δ_gap→0当c→1⁺，暗示c=1处可能存在非孤立、零间隙的CFT模空间边界。

Conclusion: 整性条件是刻画物理CFT空间的关键约束；c>1紧致幺正2d CFT的谱间隙在c→1⁺极限下趋于零，这为理解c=1的临界理论（如自由玻色子）与c<1的离散系列之间的相变提供了新线索。

Abstract: We numerically study the space of compact unitary two-dimensional conformal field theories (2d CFTs) with central charge $c>1$ using the modular bootstrap. We focus on bounding the spectral gap $\Delta_{\rm gap}$ of such theories, namely the smallest scaling dimension among the non-vacuum primary operators. We use both a primal method that directly searches for consistent spectra and a dual method that yields rigorous exclusions. A key feature of our analysis is requiring the degeneracies of primary operators to be positive integers. At $c$ very near $1$, the integrality constraint greatly reduces the allowed spectral gap in both methods. For example, at $c-1 = 3\times 10^{-3}$ integrality strengthens the rigorous bound from $\Delta_{\rm gap} \lesssim 0.5$ to $\Delta_{\rm gap} < 0.041$. Abstract: The QCD axion remains one of the most compelling candidates for the dark matter of the Universe, yet the mass range around $1$\text{--}\SI{2}{\micro\electronvolt} remains essentially unexplored. In this paper, we describe a proposal to employ CERN's M1 superconducting magnet to deploy a haloscope to perform a search for galactic axion and axion-like particle dark matter providing continuous coverage from approximately 1.2 to 1.8 \SI{}{\micro\electronvolt}. Under conservative assumptions, the experiment can reach the QCD axion band over a significant fraction of the scanned range, while optimistic detector performance would allow sensitivity to benchmark models such as KSVZ and DFSZ over a large part of the explored mass interval. With the current long shut-down of CERN's accelerators providing a unique and timely window, such an experiment will deliver high-impact results even in conservative scenarios and potentially delivering a definitive test of the axion dark matter paradigm in the unexplored $1$\text{--}\SI{2}{\micro\electronvolt} mass range.

Abstract: Data-quality monitoring at the LHC decides, luminosity block by luminosity block, which collision data enter physics analyses, yet its methods cannot be compared openly because the experiments' monitoring data are not public. We construct a public benchmark from the ATLAS Open Data release of the 2015--2016 proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$~TeV: 1289-bin histograms of reconstructed jets, electrons, photons, muons and the missing transverse momentum computed from them, for 39\,828 luminosity blocks of all 217 runs in the Good Run Lists; eight types of detector defects injected at event level at three strengths; real-data labels from the Good Run Lists and the liquid-argon calorimeter error flag; and a time-ordered protocol in which every detector is trained and calibrated on earlier runs only. Histogram shapes differ far more between runs than within a run (median $\chi^2/n_\mathrm{dof}$ of 5.9 against 1.1), so references and autoencoders built across runs fail, and all competitive detectors use residuals with respect to a robust reference of each run. A gradient-boosted classifier on position-invariant features of these residuals, trained on synthetic defects and calibrated by split-conformal prediction, detects 43\% of the luminosity blocks with injected defects at a false-positive rate of 1.0\%, against 25\% for a matched-filter scan and 63\% for a single-histogram test that knows the defect pattern. For defect types excluded from its training, its efficiency falls to 18\%; adding pattern-agnostic synthetic detector failures to the training raises it to 34\%, above the scan, at a cost of three percentage points on the known types.

Abstract: The Prompt Lepton Isolation Tagger (PLIT) is a transformer-based algorithm designed to distinguish prompt leptons (electrons and muons) produced in the primary decays of electroweak gauge bosons from non-prompt leptons produced in hadron decays and from misidentified leptons. It is primarily used in measurements targeting multilepton final states with important backgrounds from non-prompt and misidentified leptons. PLIT exploits information from charged-particle tracks associated with the lepton candidate, together with lepton properties, using a transformer neural network to identify correlations between the tracks and improve the discrimination. Its performance is evaluated using simulated top-quark pair production events and compared with that of previously used lepton isolation algorithms, demonstrating improved prompt lepton efficiency while maintaining high rejection of non-prompt leptons. This article presents the development, performance, and calibration of PLIT for electrons and muons in proton--proton collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}$=13.6 TeV recorded by the ATLAS experiment at the Large Hadron Collider. Operating points are defined for both electrons and muons, and their efficiencies are calibrated using $Z\to l^+l^-$ events in Run 3 data, providing data-to-simulation correction factors for physics analyses.

Limits are set on the resonant $X \to SH$ production for several combinations of the two scalar's masses within the $m_X$ mass range $350-3000$ GeV and $m_S$ mass range $170-2500$ GeV.

Abstract: Understanding how the properties of the visible Universe depend on the parameters of the Standard Model of particle physics requires \emph{ab initio} theoretical computations. To that end, we investigate the quark-mass dependence of low-energy nucleon-nucleon scattering in the deuteron and dineutron channels. We perform lattice-QCD calculations at pion masses $M_\pi\approx 200$ and $280$ MeV and compare their results to phenomenology at the physical point and to a previous calculation at $M_\pi\approx 710$ MeV. Scattering amplitudes, phase shifts, and threshold parameters are determined from finite-volume spectra using quantization conditions that explicitly incorporate long-distance effects from one-pion exchange. We analyze the pion-mass dependence of scattering lengths and effective ranges using chiral effective field theory with Weinberg and Kaplan-Savage-Wise power countings. From this, we estimate the pion-mass derivatives of the inverse scattering lengths and binding momentum, at the physical point. Both power countings indicate that the deuteron unbinds near $M_\pi\approx 220$ MeV.

Abstract: We investigate the role of boundary conditions in the critical behaviour of the ferromagnetic Ising model on regular hyperbolic tessellations. Using worm and Metropolis simulations, we compare open boundaries with wired boundaries implemented through a common auxiliary spin. For open boundaries, the susceptibility exhibits volume-based finite-size scaling with non-mean-field scaling exponents that are approximately stable across four tessellations, supporting the possibility of a common universality class. Comparisons with Binder crossings and susceptibility peaks reveal substantial finite-size effects. For wired boundaries, the central-to-auxiliary spin correlator exhibits radial scaling consistent with a mean-field magnetization exponent. The central-spin response to a uniform magnetic field provides a further consistency test of $\delta=3$. Abstract: We investigate the collisional quantum dynamics of an open system in contact with a thermal bath. We propose a particle-bath interaction model based on the Weyl quantization principle and the Wigner phase space description of quantum evolution. This allows us to extend the classical Fokker--Planck relaxation mechanism to incorporate Lindblad quantum stochastic jumps within the Wigner phase-space formalism. We establish the mathematical well-posedness of the Lindblad problem and we validate our approach through numerical simulations. Our model resolves unphysical long-time artifacts of standard quantum Fokker--Planck models in quartic double-well traps, accurately captures dissipative quantum state localization in 2D molecular potentials (4D phase space), and effectively evaluates electron transport and trapping times in InAs/InP resonant tunneling diodes.

Abstract: We investigate the formation and cosmological consequences of light primordial black holes (PBHs) from vacuum bubbles nucleated during a spectator sector first-order phase transition in inflation. The vacuum ordering subsequently reverses near the end of inflation, leaving the bubble interiors in a metastable false vacuum state. We examine their stability against reverse vacuum tunneling and impose benchmark conditions to suppress this process before and after reheating. In this framework, the PBH abundance is determined by the vacuum tunneling history, while the characteristic mass depends on the nucleation epoch and bubble growth during inflation. Abstract: We revisit the impact of electroweak (EW) radiative corrections on the kinematic distributions of charged leptons in neutrino-induced charged-current (CC) deep inelastic scattering (DIS). We compare our results to exclusive NLO QCD plus parton shower (PS) predictions obtained with an existing $\texttt{POWHEG-BOX}$ event generator, examining different PS models and settings. Based on this comparison, we provide recommendations for the use of the $\texttt{POWHEG-BOX}$ event generator in experimental analyses. We further assess the impact of radiative corrections on the correlation between the observed muon energy and the incoming neutrino energy, and on the mean inelasticity averaged over a realistic neutrino flux. Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) are non-perturbative inputs whose scale dependence is governed by the Dokshitzer-Gribov-Lipatov-Altarelli-Parisi (DGLAP) equations. In the second scenario, light sterile neutrinos can leave signatures through displaced vertex searches at accelerator facilities.

Abstract: The uncertain calibration of gravitational wave detectors is one source of systematic error in gravitational wave inference. While generally small, this uncertainty can be accounted for by modeling and marginalizing over calibration corrections. In this work we extend the standard frequency-domain approach to calibration marginalization to the time domain using basis splines, in such a way that the marginalization is analytic. We apply this method to the time-domain analysis of black hole ringdown, in particular the two highest signal-to-noise events detected so far, GW250114 and GW250207. For GW250114 we continue to find strong evidence for two ringdown modes, after marginalizing over the small calibration uncertainty at the time of the event. For GW250207, the calibration of the LIGO-Hanford detector was uncertain, and calibration marginalization is required for a coherent analysis using both LIGO detectors. We find that when calibration effects are included the remnant mass and spins inferred during ringdown agree with inferences from the full signal. Despite the high signal-to-noise of GW250207, we find no evidence for the presence of a second mode in the ringdown, and as a result deviations from relativity during ringdown are not well constrained.

Abstract: We investigate scalar perturbations in a two-scalar-field cosmological model unifying warm inflation, dark matter, and dark energy. Abstract: The cosmological principle may require more than observations demand: what is needed is isotropic expansion without vorticity. Method: 应用最新推导的完整微扰界面连接条件（full perturbed junction conditions），系统计算四类薄壳模型ECOs（虫洞、零表面能密度dS星、一般dS星、AdS星）的奇偶宇称潮汐Love数。

Result: 奇宇称TLNs在所有模型中均呈现多项式标度（而非对数）；偶宇称TLNs则依模型不同，可呈对数或多项式标度；且TLN符号在奇宇称 sector 可与先前结果定性相反。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the tidal Love numbers (TLNs) of several classes of exotic compact objects (ECOs) using the recently derived full perturbed junction conditions. We investigate four representative thin-shell models: wormholes, de Sitter gravastars with zero surface energy density, general de Sitter gravastars, and anti-de Sitter gravastars. Previous studies of wormholes and de Sitter gravastars with zero surface energy density, based on simplified matching conditions, suggested that the TLNs of ultracompact ECOs vanish logarithmically in the black-hole (BH) limit, leading to the conjecture that this logarithmic dependence is a generic feature of ultracompact exotic objects. We show that this logarithmic behavior is not universal once the full perturbed junction conditions are imposed. In particular, the odd-parity TLNs exhibit polynomial scaling in the BH limit for all the models considered, while the even-parity sector displays both logarithmic and polynomial behaviors depending on the model. We also find that the signs of the TLNs can differ qualitatively from previous results, especially in the odd-parity sector. Our results demonstrate that the tidal response of ECOs is strongly affected by the perturbed matching conditions and that the previously conjectured universal logarithmic scaling near the BH limit is not generic.

</details>

Abstract: This thesis investigates the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe within modified gravity theories, with particular emphasis on symmetric teleparallel gravity and its extensions, including $f(Q)$, $f(Q, B)$, and $f(Q, \phi)$ gravity. It explores the cosmological dynamics of these theories using dynamical systems analysis, stability techniques, and the Noether symmetry approach. The study examines the existence and stability of cosmological critical points, background evolution, and linear cosmological perturbations to understand the different phases of cosmic evolution. It also investigates the role of observational constraints and Big Bang nucleosynthesis in assessing the viability of modified gravity models. Furthermore, the application of Noether symmetries provides a systematic approach to identifying viable functional forms and deriving exact cosmological solutions. Overall, this thesis examines the theoretical and cosmological viability of modified teleparallel gravity as an alternative framework for describing the observed accelerated expansion of the Universe.

</details>


### [97] [Demystifying Graviton Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07447)
*Igor Pikovski*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用宏观物体的质量四极矩与引力波的耦合机制替代电子电离；工作于LIGO频段（~10–1000 Hz）以利用已知天体物理引力波源并抑制噪声；结合量子声学中宏观系统单量子能量分辨技术（如超导谐振腔或机械振子的基态冷却与读出）。

Conclusion: 单引力子探测不再是纯粹思想实验；该方案不仅首次使实验探测成为可能，更可直接检验线性化量子引力理论，并测量引力波的光子类计数统计（graviton counting statistics），从而为量子引力提供首个实验窗口。

Abstract: Detecting single gravitons is possible through the extraction of discrete energy quanta from passing gravitational waves. For decades, this was considered hopeless, with estimates suggesting a detector the size of Jupiter. Here, I explain how these earlier estimates relied on the highly inefficient electron ionization process, while the absorption through the full mass quadrupole of a macroscopic system can bring graviton detection within experimental reach. Operating in the LIGO frequency band solves the challenges of graviton sources and noise suppression, allowing gravitons to be extracted from known passing gravitational waves and detection events to be heralded by classical gravitational-wave observations. Combined with the ability to resolve single energy quanta in macroscopic systems, such as in quantum acoustics, this opens a realistic path toward graviton detection. I also discuss the general limitations of particle detection in establishing an underlying quantum theory, illustrated by an energy-nonconserving semiclassical alternative. Nevertheless, the semiclassical alternative is not standard classical wave physics, and extracting $E=\hbar\omega$ from a passing wave constitutes bona fide particle detection. This opens the way not only to the first detection of individual gravitons, but also to experimentally probing linearized quantum gravity and the graviton counting statistics of gravitational waves.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解温热膨胀中非高斯性起源，特别是有限惯性效应与NMDC对本征热双谱的定量影响，弥补局域冻结近似在不等时动力学上的缺失。

Result: 获得显式有限惯性本征热双谱表达式，揭示其动量三角依赖由不等时传播主导；给出等边极限下的本征非线性参数f_NL^int；明确NMDC诱导的慢滚压低δN转换及额外立方引力顶点；指出局域冻结近似仅捕获部分几何形变，而完整动力学对定量预测不可或缺。

Conclusion: 有限惯性不等时传播是温热k-膨胀中NMDC模型本征双谱的决定性机制；局域冻结仅为近似，需结合完整随机演化与ADM约束才能实现自洽非线性预言。

Abstract: This paper derives the finite-inertia intrinsic thermal bispectrum in potential-free warm $k$-inflation with nonminimal derivative coupling (NMDC). On a fixed Friedmann--Lema\^ıtre--Robertson--Walker geometry, the complete cubic inflaton action determines the nonlinear stochastic source, encompassing temporal, spatial-gradient, and field-dependent kinetic interactions. The retarded solution captures the full momentum-triangle dependence while retaining unequal-time correlations, whereas its equilateral limit yields the intrinsic nonlinearity parameter. The power spectrum is computed with the same response and Gaussian initial state, thereby fixing the statistical normalization. A complementary local freeze-out reduction makes the interaction coefficients and two geometric shape contributions explicit. Comparison along an evolving near-attractor trajectory indicates that unequal-time finite-inertia propagation is crucial for quantitatively predicting the intrinsic bispectrum. The analysis also derives the slow-roll-suppressed nonlinear $\delta N$ conversion, together with the Arnowitt--Deser--Misner constraints and cubic vertices that characterize the direct gravitational effects of NMDC. These results clarify the respective roles of local freeze-out estimates and finite-inertia stochastic evolution in the nonlinear prediction.

</details>


### [99] [Algebraic representation of General Relativity in harmonic gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07500)
*Jaume de Haro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在调和规范下、基于4×4实矩阵代数M₄的无指标坐标-矩阵形式广义相对论，将爱因斯坦场方程重写为关于度规矩阵𝒢的非线性波动方程，并导出引力能动量赝张量的矩阵表示及其与Landau–Lifshitz复形的代数关系，最终应用于弱场、FRW宇宙学与标量扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统张量计算中指标繁复、几何结构不显的问题，寻求一种紧凑、坐标自洽且代数透明的广义相对论表述，便于解析操作与物理直觉构建。

Method: 引入音乐同构算子(♯,♭)、度规伴随(†)及矩阵值导矢(ℬ,𝒞,𝒟^♯)，在调和规范下将Ricci张量重构为以Laplace–Beltrami算子□_LB = tr(𝒢^♯ ∂²)为主导的矩阵代数结构；将度规视为M₄中变量，推导无指标Einstein方程与能量动量守恒律的矩阵发散形式。

Result: 获得爱因斯坦方程的非线性波方程形式□_LB 𝒢 = ℱ(𝒢,∂𝒢)；导出引力能动量赝张量的矩阵表示𝒯̄，并建立其与Landau–Lifshitz复形𝒯_LL的显式代数关系；给出守恒律的简洁矩阵形式∂^T[(−g)(𝒯^♯ + 𝒯_LL^♯)] = 0^T；成功应用于线性化引力、谐波时标的Friedmann动力学及标量宇宙扰动（Newtonian与谐波规范）。

Conclusion: 该坐标-矩阵框架不仅保持广义相对论的完整物理内容，而且显著提升代数操作效率与几何结构可读性，为数值相对论、量子引力初态构造及宇宙学微扰理论提供新的代数工具。

Abstract: We present a self-contained coordinate-matrix formulation of General Relativity in the harmonic gauge, providing a compact, index-free representation of the gravitational field equations within the algebra of $4\times 4$ real matrices $M_4$. By defining musical isomorphism operators ($\sharp, \flat$), metric adjoints ($\dagger$), and matrix-valued derivative vectors ($\bm{\mathcal{B}}, \bm{\mathcal{C}}, \bm{\mathcal{D}}^\sharp$), the Ricci tensor is recast into an algebraic matrix structure dominated by the Laplace--Beltrami operator $\Box_{\mathrm{LB}} \coloneqq \operatorname{tr}(\mathcal{G}^\sharp \partial^2)$, where $\partial^2 \coloneqq \bm{\partial}\bm{\partial}^T$ represents the matrix Hessian operator of second partial derivatives. Within this setting, Einstein's field equations naturally take the form of a non-linear wave equation for the metric matrix $\mathcal{G}$, giving rise to an index-free matrix representation of the gravitational energy-momentum pseudotensor $\bar{\mathcal{T}}$. We derive the explicit algebraic relation connecting $\bar{\mathcal{T}}$ with the classical Landau--Lifshitz energy-momentum complex $\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{LL}}$, and express the associated local conservation law in a simple matrix divergence form, $\bm{\partial}^T \left((-g)(\mathcal{T}^{\sharp} + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{LL}}^{\sharp})\right) = \bm{0}^T$. Finally, we illustrate the scope and applicability of the matrix formalism through three physical applications: weak-field linearized gravity, the exact derivation of Friedmann cosmological dynamics in harmonic time, and the complete formulation of linear scalar cosmological perturbations in both the Newtonian and harmonic gauges.

</details>


### [100] [Circular motion and fundamental frequencies of charged particles in dipole magnetic field around magnetized Bocharova--Bronnikov--Melnikov--Bekenstein black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07525)

TL;DR: 本文研究了围绕静态BBMB黑洞的稳态电流环产生的磁场及其对带电测试粒子的影响，通过偶极近似求解麦克斯韦方程，得到内外径向函数；发现所有正则模在退化视界处消失，磁通量为零；分析了有效势、圆轨道、ISCO、结合能代理及振荡频率，揭示磁耦合强度对轨道稳定性与结构的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场中电磁场结构及其对带电粒子动力学的影响，特别是BBMB标量黑洞这一非平凡静态度规背景下的磁响应特性。

Conclusion: BBMB黑洞上电流环的偶极磁场导致独特的轨道拓扑重构，磁耦合可显著增强束缚能并诱导稳定性相变，但偶极近似仅在远离源处可靠；该结果凸显标量黑洞电磁响应与Schwarzschild情形的本质差异。

Abstract: We examine the field produced by a stationary current loop encircling a static Bocharova--Bronnikov--Melnikov--Bekenstein (BBMB) black hole and the response of charged test particles. From the dipole projection of Maxwell's equations, we obtain closed-form interior and exterior radial functions, and we test them against a multipole expansion of the localized source whose radial functions are also elementary. Every horizon-regular mode vanishes at the degenerate horizon $r_+=M$, so the magnetic flux through the horizon is zero. For a loop at $r_0=10M$, the dipole errors in the equatorial potential and polar field remain below $10\%$ for $r\lesssim0.42\,r_0$ and $r\gtrsim3.02\,r_0$, but reach about $40\%$ and $80\%$ close to the loop. Using the resulting potential, we study the effective potential, circular orbits, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), a binding-energy proxy, and epicyclic frequencies. Because the dipole field reverses its equatorial direction across the loop, a positive magnetic coupling $\mathcal{B}$ acts oppositely on the two sides. Above $\mathcal{B}_c\simeq0.0119$, the prograde radially stable orbits split into two bands separated by an unstable gap. At $\mathcal{B}=0.2$, the ISCO moves from $4M$ to $2.97M$, the exterior band begins at $45.46M$, and the ISCO binding-energy proxy grows from $8.14\%$ to $23.39\%$, compared with $5.72\%$ for neutral particles around a Schwarzschild black hole. For negative coupling, exterior orbits can be radially stable but vertically unstable. Higher multipoles preserve the large-coupling trends but shift the near-loop values appreciably, so the near-loop results apply to the projected source rather than to a thin ring.

</details>


### [101] [Can absorption enhance differences between black-hole images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07544)
*Tong Li,Jian-Yun Wan,Zi-Liang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究吸积流吸收效应对不同时空（如Schwarzschild与正则黑洞）成像形态差异的增强作用，发现当有效吸收系数χ₀M处于特定阈值时，图像亮度分布发生异步跃迁，导致归一化图像差异显著增大（最高达46.6%），表明吸收依赖的形态变化可为区分具有相似阴影尺度的时空提供补充诊断信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分具有相近临界冲击参数（即相似阴影尺寸）的不同黑洞时空模型，需超越传统阴影轮廓分析，探索吸积流物理（如吸收）对图像形态的敏感性。

Method: 采用现象学厚吸积流模型，引入有效吸收系数χ₀；系统比较Schwarzschild与Bardeen、Hayward、Ayón-Beato–García正则黑洞在面朝观测下的辐射图像；定量计算独立归一化及按临界冲击参数重标度后的图像差异积分，并扫描χ₀、自旋参数g/M、倾角及流动参数空间。

Result: 在χ₀M ≈ 0.585（Schwarzschild–Bardeen）等特定吸收阈值处，透镜特征亮度主峰发生异步转移，导致图像差异积分分别提升23.5%（物理网格）和46.6%（临界参数重标度）；该增强效应普适于三类正则黑洞，但幅度与发生条件依赖于吸积流具体构型；仅亮度峰异步转移不足以保证增强。

Conclusion: 吸收引起的图像形态变化可作为独立于临界曲线尺度的诊断工具，辅助区分阴影尺寸相近的不同时空；其实验验证需基于物理自洽、频率依赖的辐射转移建模。

Abstract: We investigate whether absorption can enhance morphological differences between images of different spacetimes, particularly when their critical impact parameters are similar. Using a phenomenological thick-accretion-flow model with an effective absorption coefficient $\chi_0$, we compare Schwarzschild black-hole images with those of Bardeen, Hayward, and Ayón-Beato--García black holes. In the face-on Schwarzschild--Bardeen comparison with $g/M=0.5$, the dominant brightness shifts from a narrow lensed feature to a broader outer feature at $\chi_0M\simeq$0.585 and 0.717, respectively. Near the first transition, the integrated difference between independently normalized images increases by $23.5$% on a common physical grid and by $46.6$% after rescaling by the respective critical impact parameters. Enhancement occurs across all three regular-black-hole families and over a broad range of the sampled flow parameters and viewing inclinations, although its magnitude and occurrence depend on the prescribed flow. Asynchronous brightness-peak transitions alone are insufficient to guarantee enhancement. These results suggest that the opacity dependence of image morphology may provide information complementary to the critical-curve scale, potentially helping distinguish spacetimes with similar characteristic shadow sizes. Assessing this possibility observationally requires physically consistent, frequency-dependent radiative transfer.

</details>


### [102] [Rational Orbits and Gravitational-Wave Signatures around a Horndeski Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07596)
*Wei Gao,Xiaoyan Zhu,Wenbin Lin,Siming Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有次级标量毛的静态Horndeski黑洞的赤道类时测地线及其引力波辐射，发现有效压强参数 $p_{\mathrm{eff}}$ 的增加会系统性地外移ISCO、MBO等轨道半径并改变有理轨道参数 $q$；在EMRI模型和四极近似下，预测其mHz频段特征应变达 $\sim10^{-18}$，可被LISA部分探测，从而为检验广义相对论提供强场引力探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强引力场中的有效性，特别是通过具有次级标量毛的Horndeski黑洞模型探索修正引力理论对轨道动力学与引力波谱的影响。

Abstract: We study equatorial timelike geodesics and associated gravitational-wave (GW) emission for a static Horndeski black hole with secondary scalar hair, characterized by an effective pressure parameter $p_{\mathrm{eff}}$. Using the rational-orbit taxonomy $(z,w,v)$, we analyze the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), marginally bound orbit (MBO), and periodic bound orbits. We find that increasing $p_{\mathrm{eff}}$ systematically shifts orbital radii outward and modifies the rational parameter $q$. Within the test-particle and quadrupole approximations, we estimate GW signals from an extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) system at a luminosity distance of $D_L=8000\,\mathrm{pc}$. The characteristic strains reach $\sim10^{-18}$ in the mHz band, partially overlapping the nominal LISA sensitivity range. These results demonstrate that Horndeski corrections systematically alter strong-field orbital dynamics and GW spectra, offering a phenomenological probe of deviations from general relativity.

For a nonextremal spherical horizon, we demonstrate that the positive residue of a meromorphic one-form directly determines the inverse Hawking temperature. Applying the first law of thermodynamics recovers the standard Schwarzschild temperature and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy under standard normalization. The resulting entropy defines an off-shell canonical exponent, which reveals that equilibrium thermal production of PBHs is exponentially suppressed in a radiation-dominated universe, thereby confirming that the standard density-perturbation collapse criterion remains the primary formation mechanism. For post-formation evolution, we adopt a detailed-balance mass law, prove the validity of the generalized second law of thermodynamics, and express the evolution dynamics as a free-energy gradient flow where the moving mass acts as an instantaneous evaporation-accretion threshold. Finally, we derive explicit first-order shifts for the temperature, entropy, thermal saddle, and nucleation exponent under small analytic deformations of the residue.

Abstract: Building on the fact that circular orbits are the fixed points of the phase portraits arising from the geodesic equations on a black hole spacetime, we develop a general orbital geometry, which captures the stability structure of orbits. The construction uses the orbital energy, angular momentum and angular velocity as state space variables, with response functions encoding how the circular orbits react to changes in the black hole background and in their angular motion. The associated scalar curvature provides a geometric diagnostic of certain distinguished points of the circular orbit family, including, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), where it becomes singular. Across the ISCO, the relevant orbital response functions change sign as the system passes from the stable to the unstable circular orbit branch, and typically vanish at marginal stability. We illustrate the framework for Schwarzschild black holes in asymptotically flat four dimensions, and five dimensional Schwarzschild AdS spacetimes. In the four-dimensional case, the ISCO is associated with a degeneration of the energy response sector, while in the five dimensional AdS case it is detected through the angular response. The AdS example also exhibits a separate curvature singularity at a point which coincides with the minimum temperature of the black hole background. These results show that the orbital response geometry provides a unified geometric framework for characterizing the stability of circular orbits and criticality of ISCOs.

</details>

Conclusion: 三阶极点是宇称奇几何自由度的独特指纹，可生成区别于传统 α-吸引子的可观测暴胀预言；该框架不仅统一描述已知吸引子，还预言可被 BICEP/Keck 等下一代实验检验的新普适行为，凸显度规-仿射引力作为暴胀紫外完成的有效候选。

Abstract: We study slow-roll inflation in the context of metric-affine gravity. We consider the most general projectively invariant metric-affine theory at most quadratic in the distortion and linear in curvature with no propagating torsion and non-metricity and couple it non-minimally to a scalar field. We focus on the class of $\xi$-attractors, in which a single function $f(\phi)$ fixes the whole scalar sector. Integrating out the non-dynamical distortion generates a correction to the kinetic term $k(\phi)$ which is $f'(\phi)^2$ times a ratio of polynomials in $f(\phi)$. The new kinetic term can develop poles at finite field values, where the distortion field equations degenerate. We classify the poles and derive the conditions on the effective couplings for poles of order $p=2$ and $p=3$, the maximal order allowed in this setting. We then proceed to study inflation. Second-order poles realize $\alpha$-attractors with $\alpha$ fixed by the residue and are already available in the parity-even sector of the theory. Third-order poles instead require the parity-odd sector: whenever the parity-odd quadratic couplings vanish, the kinetic function splits into two independent terms with poles of order at most $p=2$. Third-order pole-inflation, as we confirm numerically for $f(\phi)\propto\phi^2$, yields $r\propto N_*^{-3/2}$ with a tunable amplitude and $n_s\simeq 1-\tfrac{3}{2N_*}$, a spectral index mildly larger than the $\alpha$-attractor prediction and well centered in the region preferred by the current combination of ACT, SPT and DESI data. Moreover, when inflation happens far away from the pole we also find a further universal attractor, an inverse-linear plateau, with $n_s \simeq 1-\frac{4}{3N_*}$ and $r\propto N_*^{-4/3}$, testable with the forthcoming BICEP/Keck observations.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a finite dark matter spike around a static, spherically symmetric black hole, with a general power-law index and an Einstein-cluster stress tensor. Smooth inner depletion and outer truncation ensure that the density and its first radial derivative vanish at both boundaries, while a single normalization preserves the prescribed exterior mass. The resulting spacetime contains an inner vacuum gap, a matter region with vanishing radial pressure, and an outer Schwarzschild vacuum. We derive the enclosed mass, density maximum, local logarithmic slope, and matched metric without surface layers, including exact polynomial-root representations of the matter-region metric for spike indices $7/3$ and $3/2$. Energy conditions and radial stability of the constituent circular orbits constrain the admissible configurations. A sufficient bound guarantees these properties for an inner edge at or beyond the Schwarzschild marginal-stability radius and also ensures positivity of the massless-scalar potential. Comparisons at a common mass budget examine how boundary regularity and radial concentration jointly affect the gravitational response. Shadows and light deflection also depend on the global redshift and mass calibration. Scalar waves matched across the three regions with one infinity-normalized time coordinate distinguish local gap-redshift effects from propagation through the complete geometry. The smoothly truncated profile provides a controlled equilibrium model for investigating how finite support and mass redistribution affect black hole observables; its taper scales, formation, and collective stability require additional physical input.

</details>


### [107] [Parity-violating scalar-tensor theory revisited: complete basis up to $d=4$ and Hamiltonian analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08191)

Abstract: We construct complete bases of parity-violating generally covariant scalar-tensor (GST) and spatially covariant gravity (SCG) monomials up to derivative grade $d=4$. Including all derivative partitions, each description contains one $d=3$ and thirteen $d=4$ linearly independent monomials. At the level of Lagrangians, we find six integration-by-parts relations that reduce polynomial-type Lagrangians to combinations of one $d=3$ and seven $d=4$ monomials with general coefficient functions. We derive the correspondence between the two bases, retaining general coefficients and total derivatives, and establish their independence under integrations by parts. Among the seven $d=4$ SCG monomials, five contain neither the lapse velocity nor the time derivative of the extrinsic curvature. For the two remaining monomials, we perform a Hamiltonian constraint analysis with a standard Einstein-Hilbert kinetic term and a lapse-dependent potential. On the regular branches considered, the lapse-velocity term propagates four degrees of freedom (DOFs), while the extrinsic-curvature-velocity term propagates six DOFs, whether present alone or together with the lapse-velocity term. These results provide a complete basis for constructing parity-violating scalar-tensor theories with three DOFs up to $d=4$ within this setting, with the parity-violating Lagrangian spanned by six GST polynomials: one $d=3$ and five $d=4$ polynomials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解正则黑洞与可穿越虫洞在统一几何框架（black-bounce）下的经典稳定性，特别是当其由幻影标量场与NED共同支撑时，是否存在稳定的参数窗口；同时澄清此前文献中对虫洞喉部扰动分析存在的技术缺陷。

Method: 将球对称扰动约化为类薛定谔边值问题；对虫洞情形，规避Darboux变换引入的非物理解析奇点，改用基于度规与物质扰动组合的规范不变变量（Cremona–Pizzocchero–Sarbach方法），导出正则主方程；通过数值求解本征值问题确定不稳模频谱，并识别例外点（exceptional points）以划分参数相空间。

Result: 所有a>0的black-bounce时空均线性不稳定；黑洞（a≤2M）具唯一实增长模；虫洞（a>2M）存在至少一个实增长模（喉半径扰动模），当a/(2M)≳4.98时出现两个实增长模，进一步减小a则模合并为复共轭对（振荡不稳定性）；发现三个例外点分隔不同扰动动力学区域；黑洞与虫洞主导不稳模的增长率在a→2M极限下收敛于同一值Mκ≃0.10121。

Conclusion: Simpson-Visser black-bounce族在单极扰动下无稳定子集；虫洞稳定性分析必须采用规范不变形式以避免人为奇点；其不稳谱呈现丰富的拓扑结构（含例外点），暗示该几何族在经典引力中缺乏动力学鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study the linear stability of Simpson-Visser ``black-bounce'' space-times (regular black holes and wormholes) under spherically symmetric (monopole) perturbations, taking as a source a phantom scalar field with a self-interaction potential and a magnetic field within nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). The metric contains two parameters: the mass $M$ and the bounce parameter $a >0$, such that $a \leq 2M$ corresponds to black holes and $a > 2M$ to wormholes. The stability problem reduces to a boundary-value problem for a Schrödinger-like equation with a certain effective potential. All space-times under study are found to be unstable, and the corresponding exponential growth rates are calculated. For black holes the results are obtained without complications, while the wormhole stability problem faces difficulties that seem to be previously unnoticed. Thus, the Darboux transformation, used to remove the wall shaped $\approx 2/z^2$ in the effective potentials at wormhole throats ($z$ is the tortoise coordinate), creates nonphysical poles outside the throat for $a/(2M) \lesssim 4.98$. We circumvent this difficulty by using a gauge-invariant approach in the spirit of Cremona, Pizzocchero and Sarbach, leading to a master equation with a simple and regular potential. Its further analysis reveals unstable modes: the mode with a variable throat radius is always present; for $a/(2M) \gtrsim 4.98$ there are two real growing throat modes, which merge into a complex-conjugate pair (oscillatory instability) for smaller $a$. A further analysis reveals three exceptional points that separate ranges of $a/(2M)$ with different perturbation regimes. Furthermore, the leading unstable black-hole and wormhole modes approach the same limiting growth rate $M\kappa\simeq 0.10121$ as $a/(2M)$ approaches unity from either side.

</details>


### [109] [When Can Pulsar Timing Distinguish a Propagation Mass from a Weak Longitudinal Background?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08382)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱纵向背景对引力波传播质量角响应的模拟效应，并在NANOGrav 15年脉冲星计时数据构建的压缩高斯实验中量化了该模糊性；结果表明，引入弱纵向分量会显著削弱对指定源的质量假设检验能力，但增大脉冲星阵列可部分恢复检验功效；在低背景实验中，发现质量零点可在频率单元内被可靠排除，但精确分辨质量值仍受限于统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分引力波背景的横向（标准广义相对论）与纵向极化模式是检验引力理论、尤其是探测引力波传播质量的关键；然而弱纵向背景可能模仿有质量引力子的角相关特征，造成模型简并，需量化其对质量参数推断的干扰。

Method: 采用压缩高斯似然框架，基于NANOGrav 15年数据构造脉冲星计时残差统计量；通过控制假警率（FAP=10⁻³）评估不同脉冲星子阵（20 vs. 40颗）下，允许纵向分量时对有质量引力波背景的假设检验功效损失；另设低背景数值实验，扫描零/正质量与连续纵向振幅空间，计算两类质量假设间的拒绝功率与误判率。

Result: 在FAP=10⁻³下，20颗脉冲星子阵中引入弱纵向分量导致最优拒绝功率下降≥16.29个百分点；扩展至40颗可恢复≥7.62个百分点；低背景实验中，对间隔1.08%的两个质量值，某检验在任一质量下拒绝零质量的功率≥97.38%，但当限制另一质量处的误分配率≤10⁻³时，识别任一质量的功率上限仅为1.584%；表明质量截止（mass cutoff）可观测，但其在频率单元内的精确定位尚未可达。

Conclusion: 弱纵向污染构成引力波质量测量的主要系统简并源，当前NANOGrav数据对质量值的分辨能力受限于统计功效而非模型可辨识性——即能确认存在非零质量截断，但尚不能在亚百分比精度内定位其具体数值。

Abstract: A weak longitudinal background can imitate the angular response of a gravitational-wave propagation mass. We quantify this ambiguity in a compressed Gaussian experiment constructed from the NANOGrav 15-year timing data. At a false-alarm probability of $10^{-3}$, allowing a weak longitudinal component in a massless background costs at least 16.29 percentage points of optimal rejection power for a specified source in a 20-pulsar subarray. Expanding the array to 40 pulsars at the same background level recovers at least 7.62 points. In a separate low-background experiment, we allow the same continuous range of longitudinal amplitudes at zero and positive mass. Across that range and three injected background levels, one test rejects zero mass with at least 97.38\% power at either of two masses separated by 1.08\%. With false assignment limited to $10^{-3}$ at the other mass, the power to identify either mass is at most 1.584\%. A cutoff can thus be identifiable before its position within a frequency cell can be resolved.

</details>


### [110] [Torsion talks only to spin: the CMB temperature as a test of energy exchange in Einstein-Cartan cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08422)
*Fernando Izaurieta,Samuel Lepe,Cristian Quinzacara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在具有挠率的宇宙学模型（如Einstein-Cartan理论）中，背景光子能量是否随红移保持黑体谱及标准温度演化T(z)=T₀(1+z)。作者指出，在metric-affine模型中若能量交换‘普适’分配给所有流体（含辐射），则CMB光子将获得额外能量，导致观测温度显著偏离标准预期；PKS 1830−211、CO吸收体与SZ星系团数据强烈支持标准缩放律，排除该非守恒光子模型（Δχ²=13, χ²=50.8 vs 15.5）；若自旋由暗物质携带，则H(z)与T(z)恢复标准行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验挠率宇宙学中能量交换机制对CMB光子能量守恒的影响：在Noether恒等式约束下，仅具自旋的物质分量参与能量交换，而无自旋的光子应严格守恒；但某些metric-affine模型违背此原理，需用高精度CMB温度红移数据予以证伪。

Method: 利用z=0.886处PKS 1830−211分子吸收测得的CMB温度（5.08±0.10 K）、z≈3的CO吸收体及18个Planck SZ星系团温度测量，对比两种理论预言：（i）光子参与普适能量交换导致T(z)系统性偏低（7–8.5%）；（ii）自旋由暗物质携带时T(z)恢复标准演化；并结合FIRAS黑体谱约束与Planck各向异性谱形限制。

Result: 观测CMB温度数据（PKS 1830−211 + CO + Planck SZ）以高置信度（Δχ²=13, χ²显著差异）排除光子参与能量交换的metric-affine模型；其预测T(z)在z≈0.9和z≳3分别偏低7%和8.5%，与5.08±0.10 K实测值（预测4.78 K）不兼容；同时该模型会畸变CMB各向异性的频谱形状，被Planck数据排除；若自旋赋于暗物质，则H(z)偏差<10⁻⁴（z<3），T(z)完全恢复标准标度。

Conclusion: CMB温度红移演化是检验挠率引力中能量-动量守恒结构的强有力探针；数据支持Einstein-Cartan框架下的Noether约束——即光子因零自旋而严格能量守恒，从而确认T(z)=T₀(1+z)；任何使光子参与几何-物质能量交换的扩展引力模型，若违背此自旋选择定则，已被当前CMB热历史观测证伪。

Abstract: The cosmic microwave background temperature at $z=0.886$ is known to $2\%$ from molecular absorption towards PKS~1830$-$211; CO absorbers and Sunyaev-Zel'dovich clusters extend the measurement to $z\simeq3$. We use them on a question that cosmologies with torsion leave open. When the geometry exchanges energy with several matter components, which of them receives it? In Einstein-Cartan theory the Noether identities of each sector fix the answer. A sector's energy-momentum fails to be conserved only in proportion to its own spin, so minimally coupled light, which has none, is conserved whatever the torsion does. A metric-affine cosmology fitted to DESI DR2 distributes the exchange ``universally'' among all fluids, radiation included; the photons then gain almost a third of their energy. If the photons stay a blackbody, as FIRAS requires of any source set by local conditions, the model predicts a background colder than $T_{0}(1+z)$ by $7\%$ at $z\simeq0.9$ and by $8.5\%$ at $z\gtrsim3$, with no free parameter. PKS~1830$-$211 gives $5.08\pm0.10\,\mathrm{K}$ against a predicted $4.78\,\mathrm{K}$; with the CO absorbers it favours the standard scaling by $\Delta\chi^{2}=13$, and 18 Planck cluster bins give $\chi^{2}=50.8$ versus $15.5$. Under the same reading the primary anisotropies would change their spectral shape by amounts Planck excludes. If dark matter carries the spin instead, $H(z)$ moves by less than $10^{-4}$ below $z=3$ at the fitted parameters and $T(z)$ is standard again.

</details>
